# Glossary of the vocabulary

Generated by `lake exe glossary` from `Leanify.Fields.all`; do not edit by hand.
An article chooses its fields with `\usepackage[vocab={…}]{leanify}`
(`docs/vocabulary.md`). `$x$` is the object a noun or adjective is about;
`$a$`, `$b$`, … are the arguments of a notion.

## Bundles

A bundle is a name for several fields: `\usepackage[vocab=analysis]{leanify}` reads an
article with all of them, and `vocab=math` with every field. The realms are the bundles
that `math` is made of. No two fields of a bundle give a phrase different meanings,
except a notion whose meanings take arguments of different types, which the types decide
(`lake exe glossary --bundles` checks this).

## `core`

| Noun | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| real number | ℝ | a real number (`ℝ`) |
| real | ℝ | a real number (`ℝ`), as in `for every real $x$` |
| integer | ℝ | an integer: `x ∈ ℤ` (Mathlib `Int`, as a real number) |
| nonnegative integer | ℝ | `x ∈ ℤ`, `0 ≤ x` (Mathlib `ℕ`) |
| non-negative integer | ℝ | a nonnegative integer |
| positive integer | ℝ | `x ∈ ℤ`, `0 < x` (Mathlib `ℕ+`) |
| negative integer | ℝ | `x ∈ ℤ`, `x < 0` |
| natural number | ℝ | with 0, as in Mathlib (`ℕ`) |
| natural number | ℝ | without 0 (`ℕ+`) |
| rational number | ℝ | `x ∈ ℚ` (Mathlib `Set.range ((↑) : ℚ → ℝ)`) |
| irrational number | ℝ | `x ∉ ℚ` (Mathlib `Irrational`) |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| rational | ℝ | `x ∈ ℚ` |
| irrational | ℝ | `x ∉ ℚ` (Mathlib `Irrational`) |
| nonzero | ℝ | `x ≠ 0` |
| positive | ℝ | `x > 0` |
| negative | ℝ | `x < 0` |
| nonnegative | ℝ | `x ≥ 0` |
| nonpositive | ℝ | `x ≤ 0` |

## `unitsquare`

| Noun | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| distribution function on $[0,1]^2$ | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `(u,v) ↦ μ([0,u]×[0,v])` for a probability measure `μ` on `[0,1]²` |
| probability measure $x$ on $[0,1]^2$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | agrees with a probability measure (Mathlib `IsProbabilityMeasure`) on measurable sets |
| bivariate copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a distribution function on `[0,1]²` with uniform marginals |
| copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a distribution function on `[0,1]²` with uniform marginals |
| coupling | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | a probability measure on `[0,1]²` with uniform marginals: the law of a pair `(X,Z)` of uniform random variables |
| finite subset $x$ of $[0,1]$ | $[0,1]$ → Prop | a finite set of points of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Set.Finite`) |
| borel set | $[0,1]$ → Prop | a Borel subset of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `MeasurableSet`) |
| sequence $x$ in $[0,1]$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ | a sequence of points of `[0,1]`: its terms are those at the indices `0, 1, 2, …` |
| kernel | (ℝ → $[0,1]$) → ℝ | a Borel function of the terms `1, …, m` of a sequence in `[0,1]` for some `m` (a function on `[0,1]^m`), square-integrable for independent uniform terms |
| statistic | (ℝ → $[0,1]$) → (ℝ → $[0,1]$) → ℝ | a real function of finitely many observations `(U_i,V_i)` of a sample, measurable in them |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| with bounded density | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | an absolutely continuous function on the square whose density is bounded: `C(u,v) = ∫₀ᵘ∫₀ᵛ c` for a bounded measurable `c` |
| symmetric | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a function `C` on the square with `C(u,v) = C(v,u)` for all `u`, `v` (a copula that equals its transpose) |
| continuous | $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a continuous real function on `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Continuous`) |
| continuous | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a continuous real function on the square `[0,1]²` (Mathlib `Continuous` of the uncurried function) |
| lebesgue measure preserving | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ | a Borel measurable map `T` of `[0,1]` with `Leb(T⁻¹(B)) = Leb(B)` for every Borel set `B` (Mathlib `MeasurePreserving`) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ | a Borel measurable map of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Measurable`) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a Borel measurable real function on the square `[0,1]²` (Mathlib `Measurable` of the uncurried function) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ | a Borel measurable map of the square `[0,1]²` to `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Measurable` of the uncurried function, as a real function) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a Borel measurable real function on `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Measurable`) |
| with uniform marginals | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `F(u,1) = u`, `F(1,v) = v` |
| with uniform marginals | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | `γ([0,u]×[0,1]) = u`, `γ([0,1]×[0,v]) = v` |

| Adjective | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| convex [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `ConvexOn ℝ` on the class, for the pointwise vector space structure |
| concave [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `ConcaveOn ℝ` on the class |
| lower semicontinuous with respect to uniform convergence [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `LowerSemicontinuousOn` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| upper semicontinuous with respect to uniform convergence [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `UpperSemicontinuousOn` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| continuous with respect to uniform convergence [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `ContinuousOn` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| affine [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `A((1-t)C₁ + tC₂) = (1-t)A(C₁) + tA(C₂)` for the members `C₁`, `C₂` of the class and `t ∈ [0,1]` |
| convex | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → Prop | `Convex ℝ`, for the pointwise vector space structure |
| compact with respect to uniform convergence | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → Prop | `IsCompact` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| closed with respect to uniform convergence | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → Prop | `IsClosed` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| lebesgue measure on $[0,1]$ | ($[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | the Lebesgue measure of a subset of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `volume`) |
| standard normal quantile function | $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `Φ⁻¹(u) = inf {x : u ≤ Φ(x)}` for the standard normal distribution function `Φ` (Mathlib `cdf (gaussianReal 0 1)`): the inverse of `Φ` on `(0,1)` |
| conditional distribution function of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `h_C(u,v) = P(V ≤ v \| U = u)` for a copula `C`, the distribution function of `(U,V)`: the conditional distribution function of `V` given `U = u`, from the disintegration of the measure of `C` (Mathlib `Measure.condKernel`); a version of `∂₁C` |
| coupling associated with $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | the law of `(X,Z) = (1-U,1-V)` for a pair `(U,V)` with the copula `C`: `γ(A) = μ_C{(u,v) : (1-u,1-v) ∈ A}` |
| copula associated with $a$ | (($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | the copula of `(U,V) = (1-X,1-Z)` for a pair `(X,Z)` with the coupling `γ`: `C(u,v) = γ{(x,z) : x ≥ 1-u, z ≥ 1-v}` |
| markov product of $a$ and $b$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `(A*B)(u,v) = ∫₀¹ ∂₂A(u,t) ∂₁B(t,v) dt` for copulas `A`, `B` (Darsow, Nguyen and Olsen): the copula of `(X₀,X₂)` for a Markov chain whose steps have the copulas `A` and `B` |
| markov power of order $a$ of $b$ | ℝ → ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | the Markov product of `k` factors `C`, for an integer `k ≥ 1` and a copula `C`: the copula of `(X₀,X_k)` for a stationary Markov chain whose steps have the copula `C` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |

| mean of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∑ k x(k)`, defined when the series converges |
| geometric distribution with parameter $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `p(1-p)^k`, the number of failures before the first success; `0 < p ≤ 1` |
| bernoulli distribution with parameter $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `1 - p` at `0`, `p` at `1`; `0 ≤ p ≤ 1` |
| discrete uniform distribution on $a$ points | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `1/n` at `0, …, n-1`; `n ≥ 1` |
| hypergeometric distribution with parameters $a$, $b$ and $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\binom{K}{k}\binom{N-K}{n-k}/\binom{N}{n}`: `k` marked in a sample of `n` from `N` with `K` marked |
| variance of the distribution $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∑ k² x(k) - (∑ k x(k))²`, where `∑ k² x(k)` converges |
| probability generating function of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `s ↦ ∑ x(k) s^k`, where the series converges |

## `means`

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| arithmetic mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(x + y)/2` |
| geometric mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `√(xy)`, for `x, y ≥ 0` |
| harmonic mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `2xy/(x + y)`, for `x, y > 0` |
| quadratic mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `√((x² + y²)/2)` |
| root mean square of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `√((x² + y²)/2)` |
| contraharmonic mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(x² + y²)/(x + y)`, for `x, y > 0` |
| heronian mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(x + √(xy) + y)/3`, for `x, y ≥ 0` |
| centroidal mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `2(x² + xy + y²)/(3(x + y))`, for `x, y > 0` |
| logarithmic mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(x - y)/(log x - log y)`, and `x` for `x = y`; for `x, y > 0` |
| identric mean of $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `e^{-1} (x^x / y^y)^{1/(x - y)}`, and `x` for `x = y`; for `x, y > 0` |
| power mean of order $a$ of $b$ and $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `((x^p + y^p)/2)^{1/p}`, and `√(xy)` for `p = 0`; for `x, y > 0` |
| lehmer mean of order $a$ of $b$ and $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(x^p + y^p)/(x^{p-1} + y^{p-1})`, for `x, y > 0` |

| normal density with mean $a$ and variance $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `e^{-(x-μ)²/(2σ²)}/√(2πσ²)`, `σ² > 0` (Mathlib `ProbabilityTheory.gaussianPDFReal`) |
| density of the normal distribution with mean $a$ and variance $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `e^{-(x-μ)²/(2σ²)}/√(2πσ²)`, `σ² > 0` |
| standard normal density | ℝ → ℝ | `e^{-x²/2}/√(2π)` |
| density of the exponential distribution with rate $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `λ e^{-λx}` for `x ≥ 0`, `0` else; `λ > 0` (Mathlib `ProbabilityTheory.exponentialPDFReal`) |
| exponential density with rate $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `λ e^{-λx}` for `x ≥ 0` |
| density of the uniform distribution on $[a,b]$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `1/(b - a)` on `[a,b]`, `0` else; `a < b` |
| uniform density on $[a,b]$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `1/(b - a)` on `[a,b]`, `0` else; `a < b` |
| density of the gamma distribution with shape $a$ and rate $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `β^k x^{k-1} e^{-βx}/Γ(k)` for `x > 0`; `k, β > 0` (Mathlib `ProbabilityTheory.gammaPDFReal`) |
| density of the beta distribution with parameters $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `x^{a-1}(1-x)^{b-1} Γ(a+b)/(Γ(a)Γ(b))` on `(0,1)`; `a, b > 0` |
| density of the chi-squared distribution with $a$ degrees of freedom | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the gamma density with shape `k/2` and rate `1/2`, `k` a positive integer |
| density of the cauchy distribution with location $a$ and scale $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `1/(πγ(1 + ((x-x₀)/γ)²))`, `γ > 0` |
| standard cauchy density | ℝ → ℝ | `1/(π(1 + x²))` |
| density of the laplace distribution with location $a$ and scale $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `e^{-\|x-μ\|/b}/(2b)`, `b > 0` |
| density of the logistic distribution with location $a$ and scale $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `e^{-(x-μ)/s}/(s(1 + e^{-(x-μ)/s})²)`, `s > 0` |
| density of the pareto distribution with scale $a$ and shape $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `α x_m^α / x^{α+1}` for `x ≥ x_m`; `x_m, α > 0` |
| density of the weibull distribution with shape $a$ and scale $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(k/λ)(x/λ)^{k-1} e^{-(x/λ)^k}` for `x > 0`; `k, λ > 0` |
| density of the lognormal distribution with parameters $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `e^{-(log x - μ)²/(2σ²)}/(x√(2πσ²))` for `x > 0`; `σ² > 0` |
| density of the student t distribution with $a$ degrees of freedom | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `Γ((ν+1)/2)/(√(νπ) Γ(ν/2)) (1 + x²/ν)^{-(ν+1)/2}`, `ν > 0` |
| density of the f distribution with $a$ and $b$ degrees of freedom | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(d₁/d₂)^{d₁/2} x^{d₁/2-1}(1 + d₁x/d₂)^{-(d₁+d₂)/2}/B(d₁/2, d₂/2)` for `x > 0` |
| density of the rayleigh distribution with scale $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(x/σ²) e^{-x²/(2σ²)}` for `x ≥ 0`; `σ > 0` |
| density of the gumbel distribution with location $a$ and scale $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/β) e^{-(z + e^{-z})}`, `z = (x-μ)/β`, `β > 0` |
| density of the fréchet distribution with shape $a$ and scale $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(α/s)(x/s)^{-1-α} e^{-(x/s)^{-α}}` for `x > 0`; `α, s > 0` |
| density of the frechet distribution with shape $a$ and scale $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the Fréchet density |
| density of the generalized pareto distribution with scale $a$ and shape $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/σ)(1 + ξx/σ)^{-1/ξ-1}` for `x ≥ 0` (`e^{-x/σ}/σ` for `ξ = 0`); `σ > 0`, `ξ ≥ 0` |
| density of the inverse gaussian distribution with mean $a$ and shape $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `√(λ/(2πx³)) e^{-λ(x-μ)²/(2μ²x)}` for `x > 0`; `μ, λ > 0` |
| density of the erlang distribution with shape $a$ and rate $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the gamma density with a positive integer shape `k` |
| density of the triangular distribution on $[a,b]$ with mode $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | rising linearly from `a` to the mode `c` and falling to `b`; `a < b`, `a ≤ c ≤ b` |
| distribution function of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ ∫_{-∞}^{x} f` |
| mean of the density $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∫ x f(x) dx`, of a density with a finite mean |
| variance of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∫ x² f(x) dx - (∫ x f(x) dx)²`, of a density with a finite variance |
| variance of the density $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∫ x² f(x) dx - (∫ x f(x) dx)²`, of a density with a finite variance |
| $a$ th moment of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∫ x^k f(x) dx`, where `x^k f(x)` is integrable |
| moment generating function of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `t ↦ ∫ e^{tx} f(x) dx`, at the `t` where `e^{tx} f(x)` is integrable |
| median of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the `m` with `∫_{-∞}^{m} f = 1/2`, if there is exactly one |
| median of the density $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the `m` with `∫_{-∞}^{m} f = 1/2`, if there is exactly one |
| skewness of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∫(x-μ)³f(x)dx/σ³`, of a density with a finite third moment and `σ > 0` |
| excess kurtosis of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∫(x-μ)⁴f(x)dx/σ⁴ - 3`, of a density with a finite fourth moment and `σ > 0` |
| differential entropy of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `-∫ f log f` (`0 log 0 = 0`), where `f log f` is integrable |

## `fourier`

| Noun | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| trigonometric polynomial | ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ ∑_{n=0}^{N} (a_n cos(nx) + b_n sin(nx))` for some `N` and coefficients |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| $a$ th fourier cosine coefficient of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `(1/π) ∫_{-π}^{π} f(x) cos(nx) dx`, of a function integrable on `[-π, π]` (Mathlib `fourierCoeff`, in real form) |
| $a$ th fourier sine coefficient of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `(1/π) ∫_{-π}^{π} f(x) sin(nx) dx`, of a function integrable on `[-π, π]` |
| $a$ th partial sum of the fourier series of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `a_0/2 + ∑_{n=1}^{N} (a_n cos(nx) + b_n sin(nx))` |
| $a$ th fejér mean of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `(S_0 f + … + S_N f)/(N + 1)` |
| $a$ th fejer mean of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `(S_0 f + … + S_N f)/(N + 1)` |
| dirichlet kernel of order $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `1/2 + ∑_{n=1}^{N} cos(nx)` |
| fejér kernel of order $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(D_0 + … + D_N)/(N + 1)` |
| fejer kernel of order $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(D_0 + … + D_N)/(N + 1)` |
| fourier cosine transform of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `ξ ↦ ∫ f(x) cos(ξx) dx`, of a function integrable on the line |
| fourier sine transform of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `ξ ↦ ∫ f(x) sin(ξx) dx`, of a function integrable on the line |

## `information`

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| entropy of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `-∑ p(k) log p(k)` (`0 log 0 = 0`), where the series converges |
| shannon entropy of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `-∑ p(k) log p(k)` |
| rényi entropy of order $a$ of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `log(∑ p(k)^α)/(1 - α)`, `α > 0`, `α ≠ 1`, where the series converges |
| binary entropy of $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `-t log t - (1-t) log(1-t)`, for `t ∈ [0,1]` (Mathlib `Real.binEntropy`) |
| kullback-leibler divergence of $a$ from $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∑ p(k) log(p(k)/q(k))`, where `q(k) = 0` only if `p(k) = 0` and the series converges (Mathlib `InformationTheory.klDiv`) |
| relative entropy of $a$ with respect to $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the Kullback–Leibler divergence `D(p‖q)` |
| chi-squared divergence of $a$ from $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∑ (p(k) - q(k))²/q(k)`, where `q(k) = 0` only if `p(k) = 0` and the series converges |
| total variation distance between $a$ and $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `(1/2) ∑ \|p(k) - q(k)\|` |
| hellinger distance between $a$ and $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `√((1/2) ∑ (√p(k) - √q(k))²)` |
| bhattacharyya coefficient of $a$ and $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `∑ √(p(k) q(k))` |

## `optimization`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| strongly convex | ℝ → ℝ | `f - (μ/2)x²` is convex for some `μ > 0` (Mathlib `StrongConvexOn`) |
| $x$-strongly convex | ℝ → ℝ | `f - (μ/2)x²` is convex, `μ > 0` (Mathlib `StrongConvexOn`) |
| $x$-smooth | ℝ → ℝ | differentiable, with an `L`-Lipschitz derivative |
| coercive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x) → ∞` as `\|x\| → ∞` |
| quasiconvex | ℝ → ℝ | `f(tx + (1-t)y) ≤ max(f(x), f(y))` (Mathlib `QuasiconvexOn`) |
| bounded below | ℝ → ℝ | `m ≤ f(x)` for some `m` (Mathlib `BddBelow (Set.range f)`) |
| bounded below | ℝ → ℝ | after the noun: `a function $f$ bounded below` |
| bounded above | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x) ≤ M` for some `M` |
| bounded above | ℝ → ℝ | after the noun |
| minimizer of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) ≤ f(y)` for every `y` (Mathlib `IsMinOn`) |
| global minimizer of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) ≤ f(y)` for every `y` |
| maximizer of $x$ | ℝ | `f(y) ≤ f(x)` for every `y` (Mathlib `IsMaxOn`) |
| global maximizer of $x$ | ℝ | `f(y) ≤ f(x)` for every `y` |
| local minimizer of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) ≤ f(y)` for `y` near `x` (Mathlib `IsLocalMin`) |
| local maximizer of $x$ | ℝ | `f(y) ≤ f(x)` for `y` near `x` (Mathlib `IsLocalMax`) |
| strict local minimizer of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) < f(y)` for `y ≠ x` near `x` |
| stationary point of $x$ | ℝ | `f'(x) = 0` |
| minimizer of $x$ on $[a,b]$ | ℝ | `x ∈ [a,b]` and `f(x) ≤ f(y)` for every `y ∈ [a,b]` |
| maximizer of $x$ on $[a,b]$ | ℝ | `x ∈ [a,b]` and `f(y) ≤ f(x)` for every `y ∈ [a,b]` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| minimum of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the least value of `f`, of a function that attains it |
| maximum of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the greatest value of `f`, of a function that attains it |
| infimum of the function $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the greatest lower bound of the values, of a function bounded below (Mathlib `iInf`) |
| supremum of the function $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the least upper bound of the values, of a function bounded above (Mathlib `iSup`) |
| minimum of $a$ on $[b,c]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the least value of `f` on `[a,b]`, `a ≤ b`, where it is attained |
| maximum of $a$ on $[b,c]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the greatest value of `f` on `[a,b]`, `a ≤ b`, where it is attained |
| unique minimizer of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the point where `f` is least, of a function with exactly one such point (Mathlib `argmin`) |
| set of minimizers of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → Prop | `{x : f(x) ≤ f(y) for every y}` |
| set of maximizers of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → Prop | `{x : f(y) ≤ f(x) for every y}` |
| convex conjugate of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `y ↦ sup_x (xy - f(x))`, at the `y` where the supremum is finite (the Legendre–Fenchel transform) |
| legendre transform of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | the convex conjugate `y ↦ sup_x (xy - f(x))` |
| moreau envelope of $a$ with parameter $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `y ↦ inf_z (f(z) + (y - z)²/(2λ))`, `λ > 0`, where the infimum is finite |
| proximal operator of $a$ with parameter $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `y ↦ argmin_z (f(z) + (y - z)²/(2λ))`, `λ > 0`, where there is exactly one minimizer |

## `dynamics`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| fixed point of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) = x` (Mathlib `Function.IsFixedPt`) |
| periodic point of $x$ | ℝ | `f^n(x) = x` for some `n ≥ 1` (Mathlib `Function.IsPeriodicPt`) |
| periodic point of $x$ of period $a$ | ℝ | `f^n(x) = x`, `n ≥ 1` (Mathlib `Function.IsPeriodicPt`) |
| periodic point of $x$ of least period $a$ | ℝ | `f^n(x) = x` and `f^k(x) ≠ x` for `1 ≤ k < n` (Mathlib `Function.minimalPeriod`) |
| periodic point of $x$ of minimal period $a$ | ℝ | `n` is the least period |
| periodic point of $x$ of prime period $a$ | ℝ | `n` is the least period |
| eventually periodic point of $x$ | ℝ | `f^m(x)` is periodic for some `m ≥ 0` |
| attracting fixed point of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) = x`, `\|f'(x)\| < 1` |
| superattracting fixed point of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) = x`, `f'(x) = 0` |
| repelling fixed point of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) = x`, `\|f'(x)\| > 1` |
| neutral fixed point of $x$ | ℝ | `f(x) = x`, `\|f'(x)\| = 1` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| $a$ th iterate of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `f ∘ … ∘ f`, `n` times; the identity for `n = 0` (Mathlib `Nat.iterate`) |
| orbit of $a$ under $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → Prop | `{f^n(x) : n ≥ 0}` (Mathlib `Set.range (fun n => f^[n] x)`) |
| set of fixed points of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → Prop | `{x : f(x) = x}` (Mathlib `Function.fixedPoints`) |
| set of periodic points of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → Prop | `{x : f^n(x) = x for some n ≥ 1}` (Mathlib `Function.periodicPts`) |
| least period of $a$ under $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the least `n ≥ 1` with `f^n(x) = x`, of a periodic point (Mathlib `Function.minimalPeriod`) |
| lyapunov exponent of $a$ at $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `lim_{n→∞} (1/n) log \|(f^n)'(x)\|`, where every `f^n` is differentiable at `x` and the limit exists |

## `asymptotics`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| asymptotically equivalent to $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x)/g(x) → 1` as `x → ∞`: `\|f - g\| ≤ ε\|g\|` eventually, for every `ε > 0` (Mathlib `Asymptotics.IsEquivalent`) |
| dominated by $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `\|f\| ≤ C\|g\|` eventually: `f = O(g)` (Mathlib `Asymptotics.IsBigO`) |
| negligible compared to $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `\|f\| ≤ ε\|g\|` eventually, for every `ε > 0`: `f = o(g)` (Mathlib `Asymptotics.IsLittleO`) |
| of the same order as $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `c\|g\| ≤ \|f\| ≤ C\|g\|` eventually, `c, C > 0`: `f = Θ(g)` (Mathlib `Asymptotics.IsTheta`) |
| eventually positive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x) > 0` for all large `x` |
| bounded at infinity | ℝ → ℝ | `\|f(x)\| ≤ C` for all large `x` |
| bounded at infinity | ℝ → ℝ | after the noun |
| of polynomial growth | ℝ → ℝ | `\|f(x)\| ≤ C x^k` for all large `x` |
| of at most exponential growth | ℝ → ℝ | `\|f(x)\| ≤ C e^{ax}` for all large `x` |
| slowly varying | ℝ → ℝ | eventually positive, `f(tx)/f(x) → 1` for every `t > 0` (Karamata; Mathlib has no counterpart) |
| regularly varying with index $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | eventually positive, `f(tx)/f(x) → t^α` for every `t > 0` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| limit of $a$ at infinity | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `lim_{x→∞} f(x)`, of a function that has one (Mathlib `Filter.Tendsto f atTop`) |
| limit of $a$ at minus infinity | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `lim_{x→-∞} f(x)`, of a function that has one |
| index of regular variation of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the `α` with `f(tx)/f(x) → t^α`, of a regularly varying function |

## `transforms`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| of exponential order | ℝ → ℝ | `\|f(x)\| ≤ M e^{cx}` for all `x ≥ T`, for some `M`, `c`, `T` |
| causal | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x) = 0` for `x < 0` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| laplace transform of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `s ↦ ∫_0^∞ e^{-sx} f(x) dx`, at the `s` where the integrand is integrable |
| mellin transform of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `s ↦ ∫_0^∞ x^{s-1} f(x) dx`, at the `s` where the integrand is integrable (Mathlib `mellin`) |
| stieltjes transform of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `s ↦ ∫_0^∞ f(x)/(x+s) dx`, at the `s > 0` where the integrand is integrable |
| convolution of $a$ and $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ ∫ f(y) g(x-y) dy`, at the `x` where the integrand is integrable (Mathlib `convolution`) |
| cross-correlation of $a$ and $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ ∫ f(y) g(y+x) dy`, at the `x` where the integrand is integrable |
| autocorrelation of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ ∫ f(y) f(y+x) dy`, where integrable |

## `calculus`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| $x$ times differentiable | ℝ → ℝ | the derivatives of order `k < n` are differentiable (Mathlib `iteratedDeriv`) |
| twice differentiable | ℝ → ℝ | `f` and `f'` are differentiable |
| $x$ times continuously differentiable | ℝ → ℝ | of class `C^n` (Mathlib `ContDiff ℝ n`) |
| twice continuously differentiable | ℝ → ℝ | of class `C²` (Mathlib `ContDiff ℝ 2`) |
| antiderivative of $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `F'(x) = f(x)` for every `x` (Mathlib `HasDerivAt`) |
| primitive of $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `F'(x) = f(x)` for every `x` |
| critical point of $x$ | ℝ | `f'(x) = 0` |
| inflection point of $x$ | ℝ | `f` is convex on `(x-δ, x]` and concave on `[x, x+δ)`, or the other way round, for some `δ > 0` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| $a$ th derivative of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `f^{(n)}`, of an `n` times differentiable function (Mathlib `iteratedDeriv`) |
| second derivative of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `f''`, of a twice differentiable function |
| third derivative of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `f'''`, of a three times differentiable function |
| taylor polynomial of degree $a$ of $b$ at $c$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ ∑_{k=0}^{n} f^{(k)}(a)/k! (x-a)^k` (Mathlib `taylorWithinEval`) |
| arc length of the graph of $a$ on $[b,c]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `∫_a^b √(1 + f'(x)²) dx`, `a ≤ b`, of a continuously differentiable function |
| average value of $a$ on $[b,c]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/(b-a)) ∫_a^b f`, `a < b`, of a function integrable on `[a,b]` |
| area between the graphs of $a$ and $b$ on $[c,d]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `∫_a^b \|f - g\|`, `a ≤ b`, where `\|f - g\|` is integrable on `[a,b]` |
| volume of the solid of revolution of $a$ on $[b,c]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `π ∫_a^b f²`, `a ≤ b`, where `f²` is integrable on `[a,b]` |

## `risk`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| heavy-tailed | ℝ → ℝ | `e^{tx} f(x)` is not integrable on `[0,∞)` for any `t > 0` |
| light-tailed | ℝ → ℝ | `e^{tx} f(x)` is integrable on `[0,∞)` for some `t > 0` |
| with increasing failure rate | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x) S(y) ≤ f(y) S(x)` for `x ≤ y`: `f/S` is nondecreasing (IFR) |
| with decreasing failure rate | ℝ → ℝ | `f(y) S(x) ≤ f(x) S(y)` for `x ≤ y`: `f/S` is nonincreasing (DFR) |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |

| hazard function of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ f(x)/S(x)`, where `S(x) > 0` |

## `stochasticorders`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| stochastically smaller than $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `S_f(x) ≤ S_g(x)` for every `x` (`f ≤_st g`) |
| smaller than $x$ in the usual stochastic order | ℝ → ℝ | `S_f(x) ≤ S_g(x)` for every `x` (`f ≤_st g`) |
| stochastically larger than $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `S_g(x) ≤ S_f(x)` for every `x` (`g ≤_st f`) |
| larger than $x$ in the usual stochastic order | ℝ → ℝ | `g ≤_st f` |
| smaller than $x$ in the hazard rate order | ℝ → ℝ | `S_f(y) S_g(x) ≤ S_f(x) S_g(y)` for `x ≤ y`: `S_g/S_f` is nondecreasing (`f ≤_hr g`) |
| smaller than $x$ in the likelihood ratio order | ℝ → ℝ | `f(y) g(x) ≤ f(x) g(y)` for `x ≤ y`: `g/f` is nondecreasing (`f ≤_lr g`) |
| smaller than $x$ in the increasing convex order | ℝ → ℝ | finite means, `π_f(d) ≤ π_g(d)` for every `d` (`f ≤_icx g`) |
| smaller than $x$ in the convex order | ℝ → ℝ | finite and equal means, `π_f(d) ≤ π_g(d)` for every `d` (`f ≤_cx g`) |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| newton sequence for $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `x_{n+1} = x_n - f(x_n)/f'(x_n)`, `f'(x_n) ≠ 0`, for every `n` |
| secant sequence for $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `x_{n+2} = x_{n+1} - f(x_{n+1})(x_{n+1} - x_n)/(f(x_{n+1}) - f(x_n))`, with nonzero denominators |
| fixed-point sequence of $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `x_{n+1} = g(x_n)` for every `n` |
| convergent to $x$ with order $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `\|x_n - y\| → 0` and `\|x_{n+1} - y\| ≤ C\|x_n - y\|^p` for some `C > 0`, `p ≥ 1` |
| linearly convergent to $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `\|x_{n+1} - y\| ≤ c\|x_n - y\|` for some `0 < c < 1` |
| superlinearly convergent to $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `\|x_n - y\| → 0` and `\|x_{n+1} - y\| ≤ ε\|x_n - y\|` eventually, for every `ε > 0` |
| quadratically convergent to $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `\|x_n - y\| → 0` and `\|x_{n+1} - y\| ≤ C\|x_n - y\|²` |
| cubically convergent to $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `\|x_n - y\| → 0` and `\|x_{n+1} - y\| ≤ C\|x_n - y\|³` |
| contractive | ℝ → ℝ | `\|g(x) - g(y)\| ≤ L\|x - y\|` for some `L < 1` (Mathlib `ContractingWith`) |
| $x$-contractive | ℝ → ℝ | `\|g(x) - g(y)\| ≤ L\|x - y\|`, `0 ≤ L < 1` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| newton map of $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ x - f(x)/f'(x)`, where `f` is differentiable and `f'(x) ≠ 0` |
| secant step of $a$ from $b$ and $c$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `x - f(x)(x - y)/(f(x) - f(y))`, `f(x) ≠ f(y)` |
| relaxation of $a$ with parameter $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ (1-ω)x + ω g(x)` |

## `odesolvers`

| Noun | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| right-hand side | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | a real function `f(t, y)` of two real variables |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| lipschitz in the second variable | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\|f(t,y) - f(t,z)\| ≤ L\|y - z\|` for some `L` |
| lipschitz in the second variable with constant $x$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\|f(t,y) - f(t,z)\| ≤ L\|y - z\|` |
| solution of the differential equation with right-hand side $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y'(t) = f(t, y(t))` for every `t` (Mathlib `HasDerivAt`) |
| solution of the initial value problem with right-hand side $x$ and initial value $a$ at $b$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y'(t) = f(t, y(t))` for every `t` and `y(t_0) = y_0` |
| solution of the linear equation with constant coefficients $x$ and $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y'' + a y' + b y = 0`: `y` and `y'` differentiable everywhere |
| solution of the linear equation with constant coefficients $x$ and $a$ and forcing $b$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y'' + a y' + b y = h` |
| solution of the logistic equation with rate $x$ and capacity $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y' = r y (1 - y/K)` |
| euler sequence for $x$ with step size $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y_{n+1} = y_n + h f(nh, y_n)` |
| runge-kutta sequence for $x$ with step size $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y_{n+1}` is the classical Runge–Kutta step from `(nh, y_n)` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| euler step of $a$ with step size $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(t, y) ↦ y + h f(t, y)` (explicit Euler) |
| heun step of $a$ with step size $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(t, y) ↦ y + (h/2)(f(t,y) + f(t+h, y + h f(t,y)))` |
| midpoint step of $a$ with step size $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(t, y) ↦ y + h f(t + h/2, y + (h/2) f(t,y))` |
| runge-kutta step of $a$ with step size $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the classical fourth-order Runge–Kutta step |

| --- | --- | --- |

| limit inferior of the sequence $a$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | `liminf a_n`, where it is finite (Mathlib `Filter.liminf`) |

| geometric progression | ℝ → ℝ | with some ratio |
| satisfying the fibonacci recurrence | ℝ → ℝ | `a(n+2) = a(n+1) + a(n)` |
| solution of the fibonacci recurrence | ℝ → ℝ | `a(n+2) = a(n+1) + a(n)` |
| solution of the linear recurrence with coefficients $x$ and $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `a(n+2) = p a(n+1) + q a(n)` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| $a$ th term of the arithmetic progression with first term $b$ and difference $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `a + nd` |
| sum of the first $a$ terms of the arithmetic progression with first term $b$ and difference $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `n a + n(n-1)d/2` |
| $a$ th term of the geometric progression with first term $b$ and ratio $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `a qⁿ` |
| sum of the first $a$ terms of the geometric progression with first term $b$ and ratio $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `a(1 - qⁿ)/(1 - q)`, `q ≠ 1` |
| larger characteristic root of the recurrence with coefficients $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(p + √(p² + 4q))/2`, where `p² + 4q ≥ 0` |
| smaller characteristic root of the recurrence with coefficients $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(p - √(p² + 4q))/2`, where `p² + 4q ≥ 0` |

## `copulas`

| Noun | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| distribution function on $[0,1]^2$ | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `(u,v) ↦ μ([0,u]×[0,v])` for a probability measure `μ` on `[0,1]²` |
| probability measure $x$ on $[0,1]^2$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | agrees with a probability measure (Mathlib `IsProbabilityMeasure`) on measurable sets |
| bivariate copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a distribution function on `[0,1]²` with uniform marginals |
| copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a distribution function on `[0,1]²` with uniform marginals |
| coupling | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | a probability measure on `[0,1]²` with uniform marginals: the law of a pair `(X,Z)` of uniform random variables |
| finite subset $x$ of $[0,1]$ | $[0,1]$ → Prop | a finite set of points of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Set.Finite`) |
| borel set | $[0,1]$ → Prop | a Borel subset of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `MeasurableSet`) |
| sequence $x$ in $[0,1]$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ | a sequence of points of `[0,1]`: its terms are those at the indices `0, 1, 2, …` |
| kernel | (ℝ → $[0,1]$) → ℝ | a Borel function of the terms `1, …, m` of a sequence in `[0,1]` for some `m` (a function on `[0,1]^m`), square-integrable for independent uniform terms |
| statistic | (ℝ → $[0,1]$) → (ℝ → $[0,1]$) → ℝ | a real function of finitely many observations `(U_i,V_i)` of a sample, measurable in them |
| finite set of positive integers | ℝ → Prop | a finite set of integers `≥ 1` (an index set of the terms of a sequence) |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| with bounded density | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | an absolutely continuous function on the square whose density is bounded: `C(u,v) = ∫₀ᵘ∫₀ᵛ c` for a bounded measurable `c` |
| symmetric | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a function `C` on the square with `C(u,v) = C(v,u)` for all `u`, `v` (a copula that equals its transpose) |
| continuous | $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a continuous real function on `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Continuous`) |
| continuous | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a continuous real function on the square `[0,1]²` (Mathlib `Continuous` of the uncurried function) |
| lebesgue measure preserving | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ | a Borel measurable map `T` of `[0,1]` with `Leb(T⁻¹(B)) = Leb(B)` for every Borel set `B` (Mathlib `MeasurePreserving`) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ | a Borel measurable map of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Measurable`) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a Borel measurable real function on the square `[0,1]²` (Mathlib `Measurable` of the uncurried function) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ | a Borel measurable map of the square `[0,1]²` to `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Measurable` of the uncurried function, as a real function) |
| borel measurable | $[0,1]$ → ℝ | a Borel measurable real function on `[0,1]` (Mathlib `Measurable`) |
| with uniform marginals | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `F(u,1) = u`, `F(1,v) = v` |
| with uniform marginals | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | `γ([0,u]×[0,1]) = u`, `γ([0,1]×[0,v]) = v` |
| positively quadrant dependent | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `C(u,v) ≥ uv` (PQD) |
| negatively quadrant dependent | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `C(u,v) ≤ uv` (NQD) |
| radially symmetric | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `C(u,v) = u + v - 1 + C(1-u,1-v)`: `(U,V)` and `(1-U,1-V)` have the same law |
| left tail decreasing | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `C(u,v)/u` is nonincreasing in `u` (LTD in the first coordinate) |
| max-stable | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `C(u^t, v^t) = C(u,v)^t` for every `t > 0`: an extreme-value copula |
| more concordant than $x$ | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `C(u,v) ≥ D(u,v)` for all `u`, `v` (the concordance order) |

| Adjective | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| convex [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `ConvexOn ℝ` on the class, for the pointwise vector space structure |
| concave [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `ConcaveOn ℝ` on the class |
| lower semicontinuous with respect to uniform convergence [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `LowerSemicontinuousOn` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| upper semicontinuous with respect to uniform convergence [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `UpperSemicontinuousOn` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| continuous with respect to uniform convergence [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `ContinuousOn` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| affine [on $A$] | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `A((1-t)C₁ + tC₂) = (1-t)A(C₁) + tA(C₂)` for the members `C₁`, `C₂` of the class and `t ∈ [0,1]` |
| convex | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → Prop | `Convex ℝ`, for the pointwise vector space structure |
| compact with respect to uniform convergence | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → Prop | `IsCompact` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |
| closed with respect to uniform convergence | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → Prop | `IsClosed` for the topology of uniform convergence on `[0,1]²` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| lebesgue measure on $[0,1]$ | ($[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | the Lebesgue measure of a subset of `[0,1]` (Mathlib `volume`) |
| standard normal quantile function | $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `Φ⁻¹(u) = inf {x : u ≤ Φ(x)}` for the standard normal distribution function `Φ` (Mathlib `cdf (gaussianReal 0 1)`): the inverse of `Φ` on `(0,1)` |
| conditional distribution function of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `h_C(u,v) = P(V ≤ v \| U = u)` for a copula `C`, the distribution function of `(U,V)`: the conditional distribution function of `V` given `U = u`, from the disintegration of the measure of `C` (Mathlib `Measure.condKernel`); a version of `∂₁C` |
| coupling associated with $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ | the law of `(X,Z) = (1-U,1-V)` for a pair `(U,V)` with the copula `C`: `γ(A) = μ_C{(u,v) : (1-u,1-v) ∈ A}` |
| copula associated with $a$ | (($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → Prop) → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | the copula of `(U,V) = (1-X,1-Z)` for a pair `(X,Z)` with the coupling `γ`: `C(u,v) = γ{(x,z) : x ≥ 1-u, z ≥ 1-v}` |
| markov product of $a$ and $b$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `(A*B)(u,v) = ∫₀¹ ∂₂A(u,t) ∂₁B(t,v) dt` for copulas `A`, `B` (Darsow, Nguyen and Olsen): the copula of `(X₀,X₂)` for a Markov chain whose steps have the copulas `A` and `B` |
| markov power of order $a$ of $b$ | ℝ → ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | the Markov product of `k` factors `C`, for an integer `k ≥ 1` and a copula `C`: the copula of `(X₀,X_k)` for a stationary Markov chain whose steps have the copula `C` |
| markov operator of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ($[0,1]$ → ℝ) → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `(T_C g)(u) = ∫ g(y) K_C(u, dy)` for a copula `C`, with the conditional distribution `K_C(u,·)` of the second coordinate given the first (Mathlib `Measure.condKernel`): `E[g(V) \| U = u]` |
| independence copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `Π(u,v) = uv` |
| product copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `Π(u,v) = uv` |
| comonotonicity copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `M(u,v) = min(u,v)` |
| upper fréchet-hoeffding bound | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `M(u,v) = min(u,v)` |
| upper frechet-hoeffding bound | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `M(u,v) = min(u,v)` |
| countermonotonicity copula | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `W(u,v) = max(u+v-1, 0)` |
| lower fréchet-hoeffding bound | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `W(u,v) = max(u+v-1, 0)` |
| lower frechet-hoeffding bound | $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `W(u,v) = max(u+v-1, 0)` |
| clayton copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `(u^{-θ} + v^{-θ} - 1)^{-1/θ}`, `θ > 0` |
| gumbel copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `exp(-((-log u)^θ + (-log v)^θ)^{1/θ})`, `θ ≥ 1` |
| frank copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `-(1/θ) log(1 + (e^{-θu} - 1)(e^{-θv} - 1)/(e^{-θ} - 1))`, `θ ≠ 0` |
| farlie-gumbel-morgenstern copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `uv(1 + θ(1-u)(1-v))`, `\|θ\| ≤ 1` |
| fgm copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | the Farlie–Gumbel–Morgenstern copula |
| ali-mikhail-haq copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `uv/(1 - θ(1-u)(1-v))`, `-1 ≤ θ < 1` |
| cuadras-augé copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `min(u,v)^θ (uv)^{1-θ}`, `0 ≤ θ ≤ 1` |
| cuadras-auge copula with parameter $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | the Cuadras–Augé copula |
| marshall-olkin copula with parameters $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `min(u^{1-α} v, u v^{1-β})`, `α, β ∈ [0,1]` |
| gaussian copula with correlation $a$ | ℝ → $[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ | `Φ_ρ(Φ⁻¹(u), Φ⁻¹(v))`, the bivariate normal distribution function with correlation `ρ`, `\|ρ\| < 1` |
| spearman's rho of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `12∫∫C(u,v) du dv - 3` |
| kendall's tau of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `4∫C dC - 1` |
| blomqvist's beta of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `4C(1/2,1/2) - 1` |
| spearman's footrule of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `6∫C(t,t)dt - 2` |
| gini's gamma of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `4∫(C(t,t) + C(t,1-t))dt - 2` |
| chatterjee's xi of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `6∫∫(∂₁C(u,v))² du dv - 2` |
| lower tail dependence coefficient of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `lim_{t→0⁺} C(t,t)/t`, where it exists |
| upper tail dependence coefficient of $a$ | ($[0,1]$ → $[0,1]$ → ℝ) → ℝ | `lim_{t→1⁻} (1 - 2t + C(t,t))/(1-t)`, where it exists |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |

| number of $a$-element subsets of a set of $b$ elements | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\\binom{n}{k}` |

| number of $a$-permutations of $b$ elements | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `n!/(n-k)!`, `k ≤ n` |
| number of permutations of $a$ elements | ℝ → ℝ | `n!` |
| number of $a$-element multisets of $b$ elements | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\\binom{n+k-1}{k}` |
| number of maps from a set of $a$ elements to a set of $b$ elements | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `n^k` |

| number of surjective maps from a set of $a$ elements to a set of $b$ elements | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `∑_{j=0}^{n} (-1)^j \\binom{n}{j} (n-j)^k` (inclusion–exclusion) |
| number of compositions of $a$ into $b$ positive parts | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\\binom{n-1}{k-1}` for `1 ≤ k ≤ n`, `0` else |
| number of compositions of $a$ into $b$ nonnegative parts | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\\binom{n+k-1}{k-1}`, `k ≥ 1` |

| $a$ th order statistic of the first $b$ terms of $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the `k`th smallest of `x_1, …, x_n`, counted with multiplicity; `1 ≤ k ≤ n` |
| sample median of the first $a$ terms of $b$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ | the middle order statistic (`n` odd), the mean of the two middle ones (`n` even) |

| slope of the least-squares line of $a$ on $b$ for the first $c$ terms | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `∑(x_i - x̄)(y_i - ȳ)/∑(x_i - x̄)²`, where `x_1, …, x_n` are not all equal |
| intercept of the least-squares line of $a$ on $b$ for the first $c$ terms | (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `ȳ - b x̄` with the slope `b`, where `x_1, …, x_n` are not all equal |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |

| wolfe step for $x$ at $a$ in direction $b$ with constants $c$ and $d$ | ℝ | the Armijo condition with `c₁` and `f'(x + td) d ≥ c₂ f'(x) d` |
| descent direction for $x$ at $a$ | ℝ | `f'(x) d < 0` |
| gradient descent sequence for $x$ with step size $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `x_{n+1} = x_n - h f'(x_n)`, for a differentiable `f` |
| heavy-ball sequence for $x$ with step size $a$ and momentum $b$ | ℝ → ℝ | `x_{n+2} = x_{n+1} - h f'(x_{n+1}) + β(x_{n+1} - x_n)` (Polyak) |
| proximal point sequence for $x$ with parameter $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `x_{n+1}` minimizes `f(z) + (z - x_n)²/(2λ)`, `λ > 0` |

## `functionalequations`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| additive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x+y) = f(x) + f(y)` (Cauchy's equation; Mathlib `AddMonoidHom`) |
| cauchy-additive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x+y) = f(x) + f(y)` |
| subadditive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x+y) ≤ f(x) + f(y)` |
| superadditive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x+y) ≥ f(x) + f(y)` |
| midpoint convex | ℝ → ℝ | `f((x+y)/2) ≤ (f(x)+f(y))/2` (Jensen-convex) |
| midpoint concave | ℝ → ℝ | `f((x+y)/2) ≥ (f(x)+f(y))/2` |
| jensen-affine | ℝ → ℝ | `f((x+y)/2) = (f(x)+f(y))/2` (Jensen's equation) |
| multiplicative on the reals | ℝ → ℝ | `f(xy) = f(x)f(y)` for all real `x`, `y` |
| involutive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(f(x)) = x` |
| idempotent | ℝ → ℝ | `f(f(x)) = f(x)` |
| homogeneous of degree $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `f(tx) = t^k f(x)` for `t > 0` |
| positively homogeneous | ℝ → ℝ | `f(tx) = t f(x)` for `t > 0` |
| logarithmically additive | ℝ → ℝ | `f(xy) = f(x) + f(y)` for `x, y > 0` |
| solution of d'alembert's equation | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x+y) + f(x-y) = 2f(x)f(y)` |

## `convexity`

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| log-convex | ℝ → ℝ | `f > 0` and `f(tx + (1-t)y) ≤ f(x)^t f(y)^{1-t}`, `log f` convex |
| harmonically convex | ℝ → ℝ | on `(0,∞)`: `f(xy/(tx + (1-t)y)) ≤ t f(y) + (1-t) f(x)` (İşcan) |
| harmonically concave | ℝ → ℝ | on `(0,∞)`: `f(xy/(tx + (1-t)y)) ≥ t f(y) + (1-t) f(x)` |
| geometrically convex | ℝ → ℝ | on `(0,∞)`, positive: `f(x^t y^{1-t}) ≤ f(x)^t f(y)^{1-t}` (multiplicatively convex) |
| ga-convex | ℝ → ℝ | on `(0,∞)`: `f(x^t y^{1-t}) ≤ t f(x) + (1-t) f(y)` |
| star-shaped | ℝ → ℝ | on `[0,∞)`: `f(tx) ≤ t f(x)` for `t ∈ [0,1]` |
| quasiconcave | ℝ → ℝ | `f(tx + (1-t)y) ≥ min(f(x), f(y))` |
| $x$-convex in the second sense | ℝ → ℝ | on `[0,∞)`, `0 < s ≤ 1`: `f(tx + (1-t)y) ≤ t^s f(x) + (1-t)^s f(y)` (Breckner) |
| $x$-convex | ℝ → ℝ | for a function `h`: `f ≥ 0`, `f(tx + (1-t)y) ≤ h(t) f(x) + h(1-t) f(y)` for `t ∈ (0,1)` (Varošanec) |
| p-function | ℝ → ℝ | `f ≥ 0`, `f(tx + (1-t)y) ≤ f(x) + f(y)` (Dragomir, Pečarić, Persson) |
| godunova-levin function | ℝ → ℝ | `f ≥ 0`, `f(tx + (1-t)y) ≤ f(x)/t + f(y)/(1-t)` for `t ∈ (0,1)` |
| wright-convex | ℝ → ℝ | `f(x + δ) - f(x)` is nondecreasing in `x` for every `δ > 0` |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| integral mean of $a$ on $[b,c]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/(b-a)) ∫_a^b f`, for `a < b` and `f` integrable on `[a,b]`; `[a,b]` is written as the interval |
| hermite-hadamard gap of $a$ on $[b,c]$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(f(a) + f(b))/2 - (1/(b-a)) ∫_a^b f`, nonnegative for a convex `f` |
| jensen gap of $a$ at $b$ and $c$ with weight $d$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `t f(x) + (1-t) f(y) - f(tx + (1-t)y)` |

## `fractional`

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| gamma function at $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `Γ(s) = ∫_0^∞ t^{s-1} e^{-t} dt`, `s > 0` (Mathlib `Real.Gamma`) |
| left-sided riemann-liouville fractional integral of order $a$ of $b$ from $c$ at $d$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/Γ(α)) ∫_a^x (x-t)^{α-1} f(t) dt`, `α > 0`, `x > a`, the integrand integrable |
| right-sided riemann-liouville fractional integral of order $a$ of $b$ up to $c$ at $d$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/Γ(α)) ∫_x^b (t-x)^{α-1} f(t) dt`, `α > 0`, `x < b` |
| hadamard fractional integral of order $a$ of $b$ from $c$ at $d$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/Γ(α)) ∫_a^x (log(x/t))^{α-1} f(t)/t dt`, `α > 0`, `0 < a < x` |
| riemann-liouville fractional derivative of order $a$ of $b$ from $c$ at $d$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `d/dx J^{1-α}_a f(x)`, `0 < α < 1`, where the integral of order `1-α` is differentiable at `x` |
| caputo fractional derivative of order $a$ of $b$ from $c$ at $d$ | ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1/Γ(1-α)) ∫_a^x (x-t)^{-α} f'(t) dt`, `0 < α < 1`, `f` differentiable on `[a,x]` |
| mittag-leffler function with parameter $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `E_α(z) = ∑_{k≥0} z^k/Γ(αk+1)`, `α > 0` (where the series converges, which it does for every `z`) |
| mittag-leffler function with parameters $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `E_{α,β}(z) = ∑_{k≥0} z^k/Γ(αk+β)`, `α, β > 0` |

| Noun | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| utility function | ℝ → ℝ | a real function on the line |
| bernoulli utility function | ℝ → ℝ | a real function on the line |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| risk averse | ℝ → ℝ | concave (Mathlib `ConcaveOn`) |
| strictly risk averse | ℝ → ℝ | strictly concave |
| risk seeking | ℝ → ℝ | convex |
| risk neutral | ℝ → ℝ | affine: `u(x) = a + bx` |
| with decreasing absolute risk aversion | ℝ → ℝ | twice differentiable, `u' > 0`, and `-u''/u'` nonincreasing (DARA) |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| arrow-pratt coefficient of absolute risk aversion of $a$ at $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `-u''(x)/u'(x)`, where `u` is twice differentiable at `x` and `u'(x) ≠ 0` |
| coefficient of relative risk aversion of $a$ at $b$ | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ | `-x u''(x)/u'(x)` |
| exponential utility with risk aversion $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ (1 - e^{-ax})/a`, `a > 0` (CARA) |
| power utility with relative risk aversion $a$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ x^{1-γ}/(1-γ)` (for `x > 0`), `γ > 0`, `γ ≠ 1` (CRRA) |
| expected utility under $a$ of the lottery paying $b$ with probability $c$ and $d$ otherwise | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `p u(x) + (1-p) u(y)` |
| expected value of the lottery paying $a$ with probability $b$ and $c$ otherwise | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `p x + (1-p) y` |
| certainty equivalent under $a$ of the lottery paying $b$ with probability $c$ and $d$ otherwise | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the `c` with `u(c) = p u(x) + (1-p) u(y)`, for a continuous strictly increasing `u` |
| risk premium under $a$ of the lottery paying $b$ with probability $c$ and $d$ otherwise | (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `p x + (1-p) y` minus the certainty equivalent |

| mean number of customers waiting in the m/m/1 queue with arrival rate $a$ and service rate $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `L_q = ρ²/(1-ρ)` |
| mean sojourn time in the m/m/1 queue with arrival rate $a$ and service rate $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `W = 1/(μ-λ)` |
| mean waiting time in the m/m/1 queue with arrival rate $a$ and service rate $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `W_q = ρ/(μ-λ)` |
| probability of $a$ customers in the m/m/1 queue with arrival rate $b$ and service rate $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(1-ρ)ρ^n`, the stationary distribution |
| erlang b formula for $a$ servers and offered load $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `(a^n/n!)/∑_{k=0}^{n} a^k/k!`, the blocking probability of M/M/n/n |
| erlang c formula for $a$ servers and offered load $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the probability of waiting in M/M/n, for an offered load `a < n` |
| pollaczek-khinchine mean waiting time for arrival rate $a$, mean service time $b$ and second moment $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `λ E[S²]/(2(1 - λ E[S]))`, M/G/1 with `λ E[S] < 1` |

| Adjective or phrase | Of | Meaning |

| solution of the first-order linear equation with coefficients $x$ and $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y' = a(t) y + b(t)` everywhere |
| solution of the separable equation with factors $x$ and $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y' = g(t) h(y)` everywhere |
| solution of the bernoulli equation with coefficients $x$ and $a$ and exponent $b$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y' + p(t) y = q(t) y^n` everywhere |
| solution of the riccati equation with coefficients $x$, $a$ and $b$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y' = p(t) + q(t) y + r(t) y²` everywhere |
| solution of the second-order linear equation with coefficients $x$ and $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y'' + p(t) y' + q(t) y = 0` everywhere |
| solution of the euler-cauchy equation with coefficients $x$ and $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `t² y'' + a t y' + b y = 0` for `t > 0` |
| solution of the harmonic oscillator equation with frequency $x$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y'' + ω² y = 0` everywhere |
| solution of the damped oscillator equation with damping $x$ and frequency $a$ | ℝ → ℝ | `y'' + 2c y' + ω² y = 0` everywhere |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ruin probability from $a$ with target $b$ and up-probability $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the probability of reaching `0` before `N` from `k` (`1 - k/N` for `p = 1/2`) |
| probability of reaching the target from $a$ with target $b$ and up-probability $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | the probability of reaching `N` before `0` from `k` |

| probability of being at $a$ after $b$ steps with up-probability $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `\binom{n}{(n+x)/2} p^{(n+x)/2} (1-p)^{(n-x)/2}` where `n + x` is even and `\|x\| ≤ n`, `0` otherwise |
| probability that brownian motion with drift $a$ and variance $b$ hits $c$ before $d$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | for `a > 0 > -b`: `(1 - e^{2μb/σ²})/(e^{-2μa/σ²} - e^{2μb/σ²})`, `b/(a+b)` for `μ = 0` |
| expected exit time of brownian motion with variance $a$ from $(b,c)$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `ab/σ²` for the interval `(-b, a)`, started at `0`, without drift |
| transition density of brownian motion with drift $a$ and variance $b$ at time $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `x ↦ e^{-(x-μt)²/(2σ²t)}/√(2πσ²t)` |

## `concentration`

| Notion | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| markov bound for the mean $a$ at the level $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `μ/a`: `P(X ≥ a) ≤ μ/a` for `X ≥ 0` |
| chebyshev bound for the variance $a$ at the deviation $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `σ²/t²`: `P(\|X - μ\| ≥ t) ≤ σ²/t²` |
| cantelli bound for the variance $a$ at the deviation $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `σ²/(σ² + t²)`: `P(X - μ ≥ t) ≤ σ²/(σ² + t²)` |
| hoeffding bound for $a$ observations in an interval of length $b$ at the deviation $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `2 exp(-2nt²/ℓ²)`: `P(\|X̄ - μ\| ≥ t)` for `n` independent observations in an interval of length `ℓ` |
| bernstein bound for $a$ observations with variance $b$ and range $c$ at the deviation $d$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `2 exp(-nt²/(2σ² + 2ℓt/3))`, for `\|X_i - μ\| ≤ ℓ` |
| chernoff bound for the mean $a$ at the relative deviation $b$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `exp(-δ²μ/3)`: `P(X ≥ (1+δ)μ)` for a sum of independent Bernoulli variables, `0 < δ ≤ 1` |
| mcdiarmid bound for $a$ variables with bounded differences $b$ at the deviation $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `2 exp(-2t²/(n c²))` |
| hoeffding sample size for an interval of length $a$, accuracy $b$ and confidence parameter $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `⌈ℓ² log(2/δ)/(2ε²)⌉` observations for `P(\|X̄ - μ\| ≥ ε) ≤ δ` |
| realizable sample complexity of $a$ hypotheses with accuracy $b$ and confidence parameter $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `⌈(log k + log(1/δ))/ε⌉`, a consistent learner over a finite class |
| agnostic sample complexity of $a$ hypotheses with accuracy $b$ and confidence parameter $c$ | ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ | `⌈(log k + log(2/δ))/(2ε²)⌉`, empirical risk minimization over a finite class |
